- **metagene-gene-weight-inspection**: Use when a PLAGE/SVD pathway activity result is significant (e.g. current vs never smokers, p &lt; 0.0001) and you need to identify which pathway genes dominate the first metagene w by absolute weight ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/metagene-periodicity-qc-around-start-stop-codons))
- **metagene-reproducibility-assessment**: Use when you must decide which matrix factorization method yields the most reproducible metagene/source signatures, by cross-comparing decompositions (ICA-vs-ICA, ICA-vs-PCA, ICA-vs-NMF, PCA-vs-NMF) of one shared data matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagene-reproducibility-assessment))

This letter explores meta-generalization for a multiparty privacy learning model in graynet to improve the performan ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/security-privacy/metageneralization-for-multiparty-privacy-learning-to-identify-anomaly-multimedia-traffic-in-graynet) · ★4)
- **metagenome-abundance-simulation**: Use when generating realistic synthetic per-sample ASV abundances from user-supplied genome molarity values for PacBio HiFi microbiome simulations, especially when between-sample variability must follow metaSPARSim R1-preset dispersion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenome-abundance-simulation))
- **metagenome-analysis**: End-to-end metagenome/metabarcoding orchestration: analyze inputs, @prepare with all analysis skills in pipeline order, run @do subagents, write Methods and Results into Article.Rmd via methods-writer/results-writer, then render. Use when the user invokes /metagenome-analysis or asks for a full 16S/WGS analysis from data through manuscript Rmd. ([Sirius-Back/Giga_Mario](https://github.com/Sirius-Back/Giga_Mario/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/metagenome-analysis) · ★2)
- **metagenome-and-diploid-genome-evaluation**: Use when corrected long reads from multiple correctors (FASTA/FASTQ, ONT or PacBio) must be quality-evaluated on diploid (HG002) or metagenomic datasets, and reference-free QV must be validated against alignment-based ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-and-diploid-genome-evaluation))
- **metagenome-assembled-genome-completeness-estimation**: Use when analyzing genome sets of mixed or dubious quality (MAGs, SAGs, bins, SUBs) where completeness varies widely, and a completeness prior must feed into iterative Bayesian core/accessory partitioning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembled-genome-completeness-estimation))
- **metagenome-assembled-genome-phenotyping**: Use when characterizing draft MAGs (e.g. biogas reactor bins like unClos_1 at ~91% or unFirm_1 at ~60% completeness) with present/absent phenotype calls, especially to verify a manual reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembled-genome-phenotyping))
- **metagenome-assembled-genome-quality-assessment**: Use when binned contig FASTA files (MAG bins) have been produced by a binning step and per-bin completeness, contamination, and assembly statistics (N50, contig count, total length) must be estimated and summarized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembled-genome-quality-assessment--b4335ce8bdbe4c98))
- **metagenome-assembled-genome-quality-assessment**: Use when a collection of MAG FASTA files (e.g. 19,542 VMGC prokaryotic genomes from Zenodo record 10457006) must be scored for completeness/contamination and assigned MIMAG-revised quality tiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembled-genome-quality-assessment--f74a4a84e06bf487))
- **metagenome-assembly**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/genome-assembly/metagenome-assembly) · ★7)
- **metagenome-assembly-accuracy-evaluation-with-metaquast**: Use when contigs from a strain-aware metagenome assembler must be scored against known reference genomes to quantify reconstruction accuracy (genome fraction, misassemblies) across varying coverage levels or conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembly-accuracy-evaluation-with-metaquast))
- **metagenome-assembly-and-binning**: Use when starting from raw paired-end FASTQ reads of a multi-sample shotgun metagenomics study (e.g. reads.csv sample index) and you need de novo MAGs plus VAMB clusters as upstream input for MAGinator profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembly-and-binning))
- **metagenome-assembly-benchmarking**: Use when comparing hybrid vs. short-read-only assembly, or group-wise co-assembly vs. sample-wise assembly, using metrics like N50, assembly size, and MAG counts on simulated or real metagenomic time-series data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembly-benchmarking--94a0b372baf4830a))
- **metagenome-assembly-benchmarking**: Use when comparing metagenome assemblers on mock-community datasets (MP mock, GIS20) where reference genomes are known and NGA50, misassembly, and interspecies-misassembly metrics must be reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembly-benchmarking--7954016914cb6b68))
- **metagenome-assembly-evaluation**: Use when HiFi long-read FASTQ reads and a metagenome assembly (FASTA, built-in or user-provided under outputs/&lt;sample&gt;/&lt;assembler&gt;/assembly.fasta) are available and you need to quantify how representative the assembly is of the sequenced sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembly-evaluation))
- **metagenome-assembly-iteration-management**: Use when running Strainberry on a strain-oblivious long-read assembly (FASTA plus sorted, indexed BAM) and you need to control iteration count via the Hamming rate improvement stopping rule and --max-strains multiplicity cap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembly-iteration-management))
- **metagenome-assembly-mapping-ratio-computation**: Use when evaluating a HiFi long-read metagenome assembly by quantifying the proportion of raw reads and read bases that align to contigs, optionally stratified by bin quality (e.g. medium quality or higher). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-assembly-mapping-ratio-computation))
- **metagenome-bin-quality-classification**: Use when a set of binned metagenome contigs (e.g. MetaBAT2 bins from a HiFi co-assembly) needs to be assigned quality labels from CheckM2 completeness/contamination values for assembly comparison or reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-bin-quality-classification))
- **metagenome-binning-and-mag-taxonomic-assignment**: Use when long Nanopore (or PacBio) metagenomic reads at moderate-to-high coverage (≥8×) should be assembled into contigs and binned into MAGs so ARGs can be linked to de novo-reconstructed hosts rather than classified read-by-read. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-binning-and-mag-taxonomic-assignment))
- **metagenome-binning-quality-assessment**: Use when binning has produced MAG FASTA bins and you need BUSCO- or CheckM2-estimated completeness/contamination scores to gate which bins go to GTDB-Tk versus CAT/BAT, or to report MAG quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-binning-quality-assessment))
- **metagenome-community-composition-design**: Use when you need simulated long-read metagenome communities of known strain composition (e.g. 2-5 conspecific E. coli strains, Mock3 downsampling at 5x-50x) to benchmark strain-separation tools like Strainberry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-community-composition-design))
- **metagenome-contig-binning**: Use when shotgun metagenomic assemblies (contigs filtered to a minimum length of 1000 bp) must be grouped into draft genome bins and assessed for quality, taxonomy, and function. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenome-contig-binning))
- **metagenome-dataset-retrieval-sra**: Use when a reproduction task requires the HiSeq, MiSeq or simBA-5 metagenomes referenced by NCBI SRA accessions (SRS019120, SRS014468, SRS015055) as classification-benchmark input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-dataset-retrieval-sra))
- **metagenome-de-novo-assembly**: Use when preprocessed metagenomic FASTQ reads (short-only, long-only, or hybrid short+long) per sample must be assembled de novo into contigs and per-assembler quality summaries are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-de-novo-assembly))
- **metagenome-de-novo-assembly-pipeline-execution**: Use when you need to assemble individual strain-level genomes de novo from Illumina metagenomic short reads (interleaved FASTQ) and compare reconstruction quality against assemblers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-de-novo-assembly-pipeline-execution))
- **metagenome-functional-annotation-comparison**: Use when you have paired MAG and complete reference genome FASTAs with KEGG KO annotations and need to quantify how faithfully HMM-recovered annotations on MAGs reproduce isolate-genome annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-functional-annotation-comparison))
- **metagenome-functional-prediction-from-16s**: Use when the input is a closed-reference-picked 16S OTU table (e.g., Greengenes+IMG, QIIME 1.5.0-dev/uclust) and the goal is to infer KO/gene family abundances or pathway-level functional profiles without shotgun metagenome data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-functional-prediction-from-16s))
- **metagenome-functional-profiling**: Use when the input is an ASV/OTU abundance table plus study marker-gene sequences (e.g., 16S rDNA FASTA) and the goal is predicting functional content rather than sequencing a shotgun metagenome directly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-functional-profiling))
- **metagenome-overlap-graph-assembly**: Use when assembling individual strain-level genomes de novo from Illumina-type metagenomic short reads without a reference, especially for high-complexity communities with many closely related strains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-overlap-graph-assembly))
- **metagenome-prediction-accuracy-correlation**: Use when evaluating trustworthiness of PICRUSt metagenome predictions on 16S-only communities: compute per-sample NSTI on a marker gene tree and correlate it with prediction accuracy to detect accuracy decay across host-associated and environmental habitats. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-prediction-accuracy-correlation))
- **metagenome-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking metagenomic gene prediction or sequence-analysis tools where nucleotide-level ground-truth gene coordinates are needed, i.e. reads must be simulated from annotated genomes at known error rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-read-simulation))
- **metagenome-scalability-runtime-profiling**: Use when you must verify that Libra's all-vs-all k-mer scoring scales linearly with input volume, or when you need per-algorithm (COSINESIMILARITY, BRAYCURTIS, JENSENSHANNON) and per-run-mode (MAP vs REDUCE) runtime figures on Hadoop. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-scalability-runtime-profiling))
- **metagenome-short-read-assembly**: Use when the input is interleaved paired-end Illumina FASTQ from a metagenome or mock community and the goal is de novo reconstruction of individual strain-level genomes without a reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-short-read-assembly--e229d18e30416100))
- **metagenome-short-read-assembly**: Use when metagenomics short reads (not assembled genomes) are the genomic counterpart to GNPS mass spectrometry data and RiPP discovery requires contigs as input to 6-frame translation and motif-ORF/all-ORF precursor mining. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-short-read-assembly--b651301791ba5a24))
- **metagenome-simulated-dataset-benchmarking**: Use when validating a metagenome distance metric on simulated 454/PacBio mock-community datasets that vary sequencing depth, dominant-organism abundance, or genetic composition, to compare logarithmic vs natural k-mer weighting against Mash and Simka. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-simulated-dataset-benchmarking))
- **metagenome-visualization**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/Metagenomics/bioSkills/metagenome-visualization) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **metagenome-wide-association-statistics**: Use when running OMARU phylogenetic case-control MWAS and needing a multiple-testing threshold that controls type I error under the study's own clade count and sample structure, rather than a standard Bonferroni cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenome-wide-association-statistics))
- **metagenomic-abundance-aggregation**: Use when you have reconstructed SSU rRNA sequences with per-sequence read-count abundances from a metagenomic pipeline and need to deduplicate near-identical sequences at a 97% identity threshold while consolidating their abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-abundance-aggregation))
- **metagenomic-abundance-extraction-from-classifier-outputs**: Use when benchmarking taxonomic abundance estimators on admixture/food-sequencing samples where cross-reactivity (false-positive signal) between closely related species, e.g. cattle/water buffalo or sheep/goat, must be quantified per classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-abundance-extraction-from-classifier-outputs))
- **metagenomic-abundance-profile-visualization**: Use when MetaMaps per-read mappings (classification_results.EM) from long reads (e.g., HMP7) must be intersected with annotated gene intervals and aggregated into a ranked abundance profile such as COG category counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenomic-abundance-profile-visualization))
- **metagenomic-abundance-quantification**: Use when raw FASTA/FASTQ reads from a sample must be classified against a reference genome collection and species-level abundances (or false-positive/deviation metrics like Σ FP and Σ Dev) are the target output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-abundance-quantification))
- **metagenomic-abundance-table-conversion**: Use when you have a taxonomy- or function-annotated abundance table (MetaPhlAn/LEfSe/HUMAnN text output or BIOM v1/v2 file, e.g. from QIIME or mothur 16S studies) and need GraPhlAn input files for circular visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-abundance-table-conversion))
- **metagenomic-all-vs-all-clustering**: Use when you need to cluster many large whole-genome shotgun metagenomes (billions of reads, e.g., FASTQ/FASTA) all-vs-all by k-mer composition and abundance, where sketching, data reduction, or presence/absence metrics would diminish resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-all-vs-all-clustering))
- **metagenomic-alpha-diversity-estimation**: Use when QCed metagenomic profiling tables (phylogenetic clades L2–L7 or gene abundance) from case–control shotgun data are available and the goal is to test whether within-sample diversity differs between cases and controls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-alpha-diversity-estimation))
- **metagenomic-amplicon-denoising-and-taxonomy-assignment**: Use when raw 16S amplicon reads (e.g. from an SRA accession like PRJNA762466) must be denoised, feature-classified, and collapsed to genus level into a count matrix that will feed co-expression network methods such as SGCRNA or WGCNA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-amplicon-denoising-and-taxonomy-assignment))
- **metagenomic-annotation-statistics-aggregation**: Use when you have GeneMark-HM output on human microbiome metagenome assemblies and need to quantify how many contigs were assigned to species-level pan-genomes versus handled by the GeneMarkS-2 or MetaGeneMark fallback branches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-annotation-statistics-aggregation))
- **metagenomic-assembler-benchmarking**: Use when comparing how many protein sequences different de novo assemblers recover from the same metagenomic reads, or when reproducing the Plass 2–10x recovery claim on complex metagenomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembler-benchmarking))
- **metagenomic-assembler-comparison**: Use when short-read metagenomic data must be assembled and no single assembler is obviously appropriate; comparing SPAdes, metaSPAdes, and MEGAHIT by N50 lets contiguity decide the assembly carried forward. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembler-comparison))
- **metagenomic-assembly-based-gene-profiling**: Use when building gene-level functional abundance profiles from shotgun metagenomic FASTQ reads via de novo assembly and UniRef90 gene annotation, e.g. as input to gene-level MWAS case-control tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-based-gene-profiling))
- **metagenomic-assembly-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking multiple de novo assemblers on a known mock-community metagenomic sample with reference replicons available, to compare global metrics (Nx, contigs, mapped reads) and per-reference metrics (contiguity, multiplicity, validity, Lx, NAx, NGx). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-benchmarking))
- **metagenomic-assembly-evaluation**: Use when multiple de novo assemblers have been run on the same recruited viral read set and you must rank their contigs against known haplotypes with MetaQUAST metrics such as N50 and genome coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-evaluation))
- **metagenomic-assembly-gene-calling**: Use when building a body-site non-redundant gene catalog from vaginal metagenomic FASTQ read sets and isolate genomes, requiring per-sample assembly, CDS prediction, and all-vs-all BLASTP de-redundification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-gene-calling))
- **metagenomic-assembly-homology-search**: Use when you have a single reference genome (or per-species references) and per-sample metagenomic assemblies/genomes, and need to extract conserved homologous regions with flanking context before downstream strain-comparison analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-homology-search))
- **metagenomic-assembly-length-filtering**: Use when a metagenomic or isolate assembly (e.g., SPAdes 3.9.1 scaffolds) contains many short contigs that are about to be classified by a kmer-based method (PlasFlow) and results for short sequences would otherwise be unreliable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-length-filtering))
- **metagenomic-assembly-quality-assessment**: Use when comparing metagenomic assemblers on the same Illumina read set (mock or simulated viral/bacterial communities) and you need genome fraction, misassembly, and per-100 kb error metrics against reference genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-quality-assessment))
- **metagenomic-assembly-quality-validation**: Use when a merged or pooled metagenomic contig set (e.g. from MeGAMerge) must be quality-checked against raw reads and known reference genomes to confirm contig reduction, coverage maintenance, chimera counts, and removal of unmapped contigs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-quality-validation))
- **metagenomic-assembly-retrieval**: Use when reproducing plasmidome classification benchmarks that require downloading the four reference plasmidome assemblies and filtering contigs to &gt;1000 bp before running kmer-based plasmid/chromosome classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-retrieval))
- **metagenomic-assembly-scaffolding**: Use when raw sequencing reads (FASTQ) must be turned into a filtered scaffold/contig FASTA before kmer-signature-based classification (e.g. PlasFlow plasmid prediction) on metagenomic or small-genome assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-scaffolding))
- **metagenomic-assembly-strain-comparison**: Use when tracking shared or closely related strains between pairs of metagenomic assemblies (or genomes) of the same species, especially when structural variation, low-data contexts (phages/plasmids), or SNP-insensitivity is required rather than popANI/SNP counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-assembly-strain-comparison))
- **metagenomic-bam-position-depth-profiling**: Use when you have a sorted .bam mapped to a reference .fasta and need per-scaffold coverage, breadth, expected breadth, and nucleotide-diversity metrics for genome populations in a metagenome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-bam-position-depth-profiling))
- **metagenomic-benchmark-result-comparison**: Reference for evaluating whether a new k-mer/minhash-based read classifier beats established metagenomics tools (CLARK, Kraken2, Kraken2+Bracken) on FP rate and abundance estimation accuracy over the same reference genome database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-benchmark-result-comparison))
- **metagenomic-bgc-annotation**: Use when you have assembled genomes or metagenomes (e.g., 40 cyanobacterial samples from the PoDP) and need annotated BGC GenBank files plus a BGC-BGC similarity matrix to link metabolites to gene clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-bgc-annotation))
- **metagenomic-bin-quality-assessment**: Use when bins from one or more metagenomic binners must be quantitatively compared on simulated data with known reference genomes, e.g. counting high-quality bins per binner per CAMI II environment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-bin-quality-assessment))
- **metagenomic-binner-benchmarking**: Use when comparing metagenomic binning tools on real short-read (human gut, dog gut, ocean, soil) or long-read (human gut, sheep gut, activated sludge) projects to quantify gains in high-quality bin reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-binner-benchmarking))
- **metagenomic-binning-and-scaffold-clustering**: Use when a hybrid metagenome assembly (Illumina paired-end plus ONT/PacBio long reads or mate pairs) must be partitioned into species-specific scaffold clusters, or when near-complete genomes and biologically important elements must be detected per bin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-binning-and-scaffold-clustering))
- **metagenomic-biomarker-visualization**: Use when you have LEfSe biomarker results and a MetaPhlAn (or HUMAnN) abundance profile and need a publication-quality circular cladogram highlighting discriminatory taxa with metadata overlays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-biomarker-visualization))
- **metagenomic-catalogue-construction**: Use when short-read metagenomic (FASTQ.GZ) data from mixed microbial communities must be mined for novel insertion sequences and consolidated into a non-redundant FASTA catalogue for downstream annotation or genome querying. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-catalogue-construction))
- **metagenomic-cds-dereplication**: Use when building a body-site non-redundant gene catalog from a large set of predicted CDSs (nucleotide FASTA) and redundant near-identical sequences across metagenomes must be collapsed into cluster representatives for downstream annotation and read mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-cds-dereplication))
- **metagenomic-clade-association-testing**: Use when QCed shotgun metagenomic FASTQ data from case and control cohorts are available and per-clade disease association statistics (p-values/FDR) are needed via phylogenetic profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-clade-association-testing))
- **metagenomic-classifier-benchmarking**: Use when comparing taxonomic classification accuracy (sensitivity/precision) of metagenomic classifiers on simulated read sets with known origins, e.g. Kraken vs BLAST-based or k-mer-based classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-classifier-benchmarking--f947bc0ce47fcc49))
- **metagenomic-classifier-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how a taxonomic classifier behaves on reads from species missing from its database, i.e. when quantifying false positives caused by similarity to the closest available relative rather than true species presence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-classifier-benchmarking--15c1de020b06ec4a))
- **metagenomic-classifier-database-building**: Use when building a custom Kraken taxonomy database from a set of reference genomes (e.g. NCBI RefSeq complete microbial genomes) so every k-mer maps to an LCA taxonomic label. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-classifier-database-building))
- **metagenomic-composition-estimation**: Use when long-read (PacBio/Oxford Nanopore) metagenomic FASTQ reads must be assigned per-read to taxa and converted into strain- or species-level sample composition estimates against a reference database such as miniSeq+H/RefSeq. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenomic-composition-estimation))
- **metagenomic-consensus-genome-assembly**: Use when nanopore FASTQ/FAST5/POD5 sample folders must be turned into per-sample viral consensus genomes with taxonomic labels (KMA → assembly → Kraken2 → Krona) as part of the VirDetector pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-consensus-genome-assembly))
- **metagenomic-contamination-assessment**: Use when validating that a custom marker-gene or reference database is specific to its target kingdom by measuring how many of its sequences align against a large out-group reference catalog (e.g., fungal markers vs. a bacterial gut genome catalog). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contamination-assessment))
- **metagenomic-contig-binning**: Use when starting from a metagenomic de Bruijn-graph assembly (FASTA contigs &gt;=500 bp) plus the trimmed reads, and the goal is a bin-quality table of completeness and strain heterogeneity per bin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-binning--7530fd71afe1cdc5))
- **metagenomic-contig-binning**: Use when starting from paired-end metagenomic shotgun reads (e.g. FASTQ) and non-redundant MAG sets are needed as input for downstream MAG taxonomy classification, especially for benchmarking classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-binning--aa6be0a34a35d65a))
- **metagenomic-contig-classification**: Use when a metagenomic or plasmidome assembly (FASTA contigs/scaffolds) needs plasmid-vs-chromosome assignment with initial phylum-level taxonomy, and kmer-signature classification without prior knowledge of sample composition is appropriate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-classification))
- **metagenomic-contig-clustering-by-organism**: Use when metagenomic shotgun reads/contigs must be grouped by organism of origin before downstream per-group model fitting, e.g. to gate retraining of gene prediction IMMs within each cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-clustering-by-organism))
- **metagenomic-contig-colinearity-analysis**: Use when a post-assembly contig set (e.g., MergedContigs.fasta from MeGAMerge) contains very large contigs (hundreds of kb) whose contiguity must be confirmed by whole-contig colinear alignment to a single reference genome, such as Supplementary Figures S4-S5 validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-colinearity-analysis))
- **metagenomic-contig-depth-estimation**: Use when you have assembled metagenomic contigs plus per-sample short-read FASTQs and need per-contig mean sequencing depths across samples to feed co-abundance-based binning (e.g., MetaBAT2) or to estimate MAG abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-depth-estimation))
- **metagenomic-contig-dereplication**: Use when multiple assembled metagenome contig FASTA files (e.g. from SOAPdenovo, CLC Bio, Velvet, Newbler) must be pooled and deduplicated prior to OLC-based merging, and exact duplicates, reverse complements, or subsequences must be eliminated first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-dereplication))
- **metagenomic-contig-extension**: Use when metaSPAdes or another de novo assembler has produced viral metagenomic seed contigs of only a few kb and the goal is to lengthen them with paired or unpaired FASTQ reads while checking accuracy against NCBI viral reference sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-extension))
- **metagenomic-contig-set-merging**: Use when you have multiple FASTA files of assembled contigs from different assemblers (e.g., Velvet, SOAPdenovo, IDBA, Ray, CLC Bio) or related metagenome samples and want a single improved, more contiguous merged contig set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-set-merging))
- **metagenomic-contig-taxonomy-annotation**: Use when you need to classify assembled metagenomic contigs as putative viral sequences and need a reproducible confidence label based on agreement across multiple detection tools rather than a single-tool call. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-contig-taxonomy-annotation))
- **metagenomic-count-filtering**: Use when a mapped gene frequency count table from a mapping-based metagenomic pipeline is sparse and compositional and must be filtered, length-corrected and CSS-normalized before taxonomic or functional (KO/EC/reaction) profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-count-filtering))
- **metagenomic-count-normalization**: Use when the input is a sparse, high-throughput marker-gene (OTU/species/taxon) count matrix with two or more sample groups, and per-feature differential abundance statistics must account for both normalization and under-sampling of microbial communities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-count-normalization))
- **metagenomic-database-construction**: Use when constructing a k-mer/minhash classification database from large multi-kingdom reference genome collections where full-database build exceeds workstation memory, or when benchmarking build time and peak RAM across partition/executor counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-database-construction))
- **metagenomic-de-novo-assembly**: Use when metagenomic Illumina (or 454) reads are available, multiple de novo assemblies (e.g. different kmer values or assemblers) have been generated, and improved contiguity (larger contigs, more genes) is needed beyond any single assembler run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-de-novo-assembly))
- **metagenomic-de-novo-assembly**: Use when raw or preprocessed paired-end metagenome FASTQ reads must be assembled into contigs de novo as the first step toward building or reproducing a gene catalog such as VIRGO, and assembly statistics are the deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-de-novo-assembly))
- **metagenomic-differential-abundance-analysis**: Use when comparing metagenomic abundance profiles (taxon/gene/KO/EC/reaction tables) across phenotype groups (e.g. healthy vs adenoma vs carcinoma) to find significantly changed biomarker features while correcting compositional bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-differential-abundance-analysis))
- **metagenomic-distance-behavior-validation**: Use when you need to reproduce, reimplement, or validate scalable all-vs-all metagenome distance computation from k-mer frequency vectors, or sanity-check that a distance implementation yields near-zero self-distance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-distance-behavior-validation))
- **metagenomic-fastq-accession-handling**: Use when reproducing a k-mer abundance indexing/AMQ benchmark that requires concrete public metagenomic FASTQ runs — one dataset to build the index and an independent query dataset for FPR measurement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-fastq-accession-handling))
- **metagenomic-fecal-source-tracking**: Use when gzipped FASTQ shotgun metagenomes from water samples must be attributed to specific fecal source categories and apportioned by cell fraction, without sequencing the potential sources themselves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-fecal-source-tracking))
- **metagenomic-flanking-region-extraction**: Use when short-read metagenomic FASTQ data must yield the genomic context (flanking regions) around ARGs or other target genes, and full de novo assembly would be computationally prohibitive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-flanking-region-extraction))
- **metagenomic-functional-annotation**: Use when you have shotgun metagenomics sequencing data (e.g. FASTQ from fecal samples) and need functional pathway enrichment comparisons between groups rather than only taxonomic composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-functional-annotation))
- **metagenomic-functional-annotation-level-comparison**: Decision support for choosing between gene-level and functional-level (KO/EC/reaction) differential analysis of mapped metagenomic count data, when you have per-level significance ratios from a case-control dataset such as CAMAMED's colorectal adenoma/carcinoma (CAMAMED) run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-functional-annotation-level-comparison))
- **metagenomic-functional-annotation-pipeline-configuration**: Use when installing MetaLAFFA or customizing its pipeline operation — choosing steps, per-step parameters, cluster submission settings, or reference databases (UniRef90, KEGG mappings, host decoys) — prior to annotating shotgun metagenomic FASTQ data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-functional-annotation-pipeline-configuration))
- **metagenomic-functional-pathway-profiling**: Use when shotgun metagenomic case-control data (e.g. SCZ n=344, T2D n=171) require pathway-level enrichment testing and integration with host GWAS summary statistics to evaluate pathway overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-functional-pathway-profiling))
- **metagenomic-functional-profiling**: Use when metagenomic reads have already been mapped to a non-redundant gene catalog yielding per-sample gene frequencies, and the goal is to extend these into KO, EC number and reaction annotations with per-sample functional frequencies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-functional-profiling))
- **metagenomic-gene-catalogue-processing**: Use when a metagenomic analysis needs gene clusters grouped by genomic adjacency (synteny) to associate functional content and shared pathways with MAG clusters or subspecies clades. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-gene-catalogue-processing))
- **metagenomic-gene-cluster-functional-summarization**: Use when you have MAGinator synteny_clusters.tab output plus a non-redundant gene catalogue and need to attach KEGG module assignments per synteny cluster and measure functional coherence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-gene-cluster-functional-summarization))
- **metagenomic-gene-cluster-mining**: Use when starting from microbial genome assemblies and needing predicted MGCs/BGCs reduced to representative GCFs as mapping targets for metagenomic or metatranscriptomic abundance analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-gene-cluster-mining))
- **metagenomic-gene-count-matrix-curation**: Use when profiling de novo MAG clusters from shotgun metagenomics requires cluster-specific signature genes: you have a gene read-count matrix (small_gene_count_matrix.tsv) and MAG cluster assignments (metagenomicspecies.tab) and need abundance-grade gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-gene-count-matrix-curation))
- **metagenomic-gene-prediction**: Use when comparing merged (MeGAMerge) vs individual metagenomic assemblies by predicted gene counts and coding density, or when annotating contigs &gt;=2 kb without a reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-gene-prediction))
- **metagenomic-gene-prediction**: Use when shotgun metagenomic reads or scaftigs (FASTA) lack gene annotations and gene coordinates are required as input for operon/proximon prediction (e.g. MetaRon 'ago' or 'op' mode). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-gene-prediction))
- **metagenomic-gene-prediction**: Use when input is unassembled metagenomic DNA reads (e.g. Sanger, 454, or Illumina shotgun reads) and the goal is nucleotide-level gene prediction with sensitivity/precision scoring against known gene coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-gene-prediction--3dbf31981d0642c4))
- **metagenomic-gene-prediction**: Use when you have assembled genome FASTA files (MAGs, fungal genomes, viral genomes/vOTUs) and need translated protein-coding gene sequences as input for downstream gene clustering, annotation or gene catalogue construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-gene-prediction--ef6aec22fbe77499))
- **metagenomic-gene-prediction**: Use when input is a metagenome assembly FASTA (human microbiome contigs) needing gene prediction in GTF format, and near-complete-genome accuracy is required for short contigs without genomic context. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-gene-prediction--75131d0769cc3eb6))
- **metagenomic-gene-to-ortholog-mapping**: Use when you have a gene-level (e.g. UniRef90-based, length-normalized) abundance table and a gene-to-KO mapping file, and need a KO-level functional profile per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-gene-to-ortholog-mapping))
- **metagenomic-genome-assembly**: Use when processing shotgun viral metagenomic FASTQ reads in ViroProfiler and you need per-sample assembled contigs plus a single species-level non-redundant contig library (nrclib.fasta) for downstream annotation, taxonomy assignment, and analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-genome-assembly))
- **metagenomic-genome-dereplication**: Use when a genome set assembled/binned from metagenomes will be used as a concatenated mapping reference or for cross-tool strain-sharing benchmarks, and redundant near-identical genomes (e.g. &gt;98% ANI) must be collapsed first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-genome-dereplication))
- **metagenomic-genome-size-estimation**: Use when metagenomic read-mapping results (per-genome sequence depth X from CoverM against source-associated genome databases) must be normalized to genome equivalents to express source signal as approximate cell fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-genome-size-estimation))
- **metagenomic-hic-binning-benchmarking**: Use when comparing viral binning tools on metagenomic Hi-C datasets (shotgun + Hi-C FASTQ pairs from SRA/ENA) and evaluating recovered vMAGs by completeness categories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-hic-binning-benchmarking))
- **metagenomic-hic-multi-dataset-quality-benchmarking**: Use when you have cleaned Hi-C paired-end FASTQ reads from several metagenomic Hi-C datasets and need a reference-free, quantitative measure of ligation/library quality comparable across datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-hic-multi-dataset-quality-benchmarking))
- **metagenomic-hit-summarization**: Use when you have filtered read-to-gene DIAMOND alignment hits ( e-value per read) from shotgun metagenomic data and need to compute per-gene abundance counts, including fractional assignment of multi-mapping reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-hit-summarization))
- **metagenomic-import**: Import WGS/metagenomic Bracken reports into a complete phyloseq object (tax, otu, sam, tree) with host cleanup. Use when importing Bracken/Kraken reports, building WGS phyloseq, or when the user mentions metagenomic-import. ([Sirius-Back/Giga_Mario](https://github.com/Sirius-Back/Giga_Mario/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/metagenomic-import) · ★2)
- **metagenomic-is-candidate-screening**: Use when paired-end short-read metagenomic reads have already been screened for inverted repeats by pal-MEM (LCoord/RCoord annotated FASTA) and sample assemblies exist, and you need proximity-filtered candidate ITR reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-is-candidate-screening))
- **metagenomic-krona-chart**: Analyze data with `metagenomic-krona-chart` using a reproducible workflow, explicit validation, and structured outputs for review-ready interpretation. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Data%20Analysis/metagenomic-krona-chart) · ★2.1k · 2 copies)
- **metagenomic-marker-consensus-sequencing**: Use when MetaPhlAn 4 profiles detect a target species (including taxonomically unidentified SGBs) across many shotgun metagenomes and you need a strain phylogeny or strain-sharing/transmission analysis from marker alignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenomic-marker-consensus-sequencing))
- **metagenomic-marker-database-construction**: Use when building or reproducing a reference marker-gene database from a large genome catalog (isolates plus MAGs), e.g. deriving SGBs and unique markers underlying MetaPhlAn 4-style taxonomic profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenomic-marker-database-construction))
- **metagenomic-metadata-regression-modeling**: Use when you have a microbial feature abundance table (taxonomic or functional profiles) plus sample metadata (e.g. geography, health status, extraction method) and need significant feature–variable associations from multivariable linear models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-metadata-regression-modeling))
- **metagenomic-mock-data-simulation**: Use when a reproducible mock compositional abundance table (with matched control/disease phenotype data) is needed to test or benchmark metagenomic feature-enrichment or causality-inference methods such as VTwins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-mock-data-simulation))
- **metagenomic-multi-sample-contig-binning**: Use when you have metagenomic short-read samples from multiple environments and want to bin contigs across samples jointly (multi-sample mode), comparing or replacing single-sample binners like VAMB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-multi-sample-contig-binning))
- **metagenomic-pipeline-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify wall-clock time, peak memory footprint, and CPU-hour usage of a Snakemake-based metagenomic pipeline (e.g. ARGprofiler with fastp, KMA, ARGextender, SPAdes, Mash rules) under its final parameter settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-pipeline-benchmarking))
- **metagenomic-preprocessing**: Use when raw shotgun metagenomic FASTQs must be cleaned before functional annotation — removing human/host reads, PCR or optical duplicates, and low-quality or too-short reads prior to gene mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-preprocessing))
- **metagenomic-preprocessing-pipeline**: Use when raw metagenomic samples arrive as SRA archives and must be converted to FASTQ/FASTA, quality-checked, and characterized with per-sample sequence statistics before taxonomic/functional mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-preprocessing-pipeline))
- **metagenomic-profiler-versus-ground-truth-comparison**: Use when benchmarking a taxonomic profiler against a known ground truth (e.g. CAMI marmgCAMI2_short_read_sample_0) and you need clade-level presence/absence discordance beyond F1 or UniFrac scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-profiler-versus-ground-truth-comparison))
- **metagenomic-protein-assembly**: Use when the goal is to maximize protein sequence recovery from complex metagenomic or metatranscriptomic short-read data (paired-end FASTQ or single-end FASTA/FASTQ) by assembling directly at the protein level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-protein-assembly))
- **metagenomic-protein-database-construction**: Use when analyzing metaproteomics tandem MS/MS spectra against metagenome-derived protein databases (metapeptide or non-redundant environmental FASTA), or when benchmarking database search vs de novo peptide-detection counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-protein-database-construction))
- **metagenomic-protein-sequence-screening**: Use when you need to identify and type-classify proteins (e.g. DsrA/DsrB and paralogous families) in thousands of (meta)genomic protein sequences in FASTA format, at scale, without phylogenetic reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-protein-sequence-screening))
- **metagenomic-read-alignment**: Use when shotgun metagenomic FASTQ reads (e.g., the 30-sample BIO-ML set from PRJNA544527 or a 121-sample H. pylori eradication cohort) must be mapped to a reference database to generate per-sample BAMs for downstream variant detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-alignment--0ff8eb41608d57b8))
- **metagenomic-read-alignment**: Use when paired-end shotgun metagenomic FASTQ reads must be mapped to a combined microbiome reference FASTA while preserving junction pairs mapping across different references, as the first stage of LEMON's HGT detection pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-alignment--d704e61d4a7792f3))
- **metagenomic-read-alignment-to-gene-catalog**: Use when you have QC'd shotgun metagenomic FASTQ reads and a non-redundant gene catalog (e.g., the IGC catalog), and you need per-sample gene frequencies as input to compositional differential-abundance analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-alignment-to-gene-catalog))
- **metagenomic-read-alignment-to-genome-databases**: Use when assigning shotgun metagenomic reads from water or mesocosm samples (e.g. FASTQ reads from PRJNA1161616) to individual genomes in a source-associated database to quantify per-genome and per-source-category signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-alignment-to-genome-databases))
- **metagenomic-read-alignment-to-operons**: Use when you have predicted operon sequences from metagenomic shotgun samples and raw paired-end reads, and need per-sample pathway/KO abundances to compare disease versus control groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-read-alignment-to-operons))
- **metagenomic-read-alignment-to-viral-scaffolds**: Use when predicted viral scaffolds (e.g., from VIBRANT or VirSorter) must be binned from metagenomes and per-sample coverage tables are needed as input to vRhyme's coverage variance / effect-size binning network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-alignment-to-viral-scaffolds))
- **metagenomic-read-classification**: Use when classifying high-throughput sequencing reads (Illumina FASTQ, single- or paired-end) from metagenomic whole-genome sequencing to NCBI taxa, especially when reference genomes are incomplete or distant strains must still be classified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-classification--0125699dfd80a26e))
- **metagenomic-read-classification**: Use when classifying shotgun FASTQ/FASTA reads from complex biological samples (e.g., calibrator sausage mixtures) against large reference genome collections to detect ingredient/species composition and estimate abundances with low false-positive rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-classification--57975e9d8b43856e))
- **metagenomic-read-classification**: Use when classifying paired-end metagenomic reads (e.g. simulated setA2/setB2 or HMP metagenome SRR1804065) against a reference genome FASTA in alignment-free fashion, computing sensitivity/precision/F1/specificity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-classification--15d0d08672305c9c))
- **metagenomic-read-classification**: Use when classifying fastq/fasta metagenomic reads at species or genus rank, especially gut microbiome reads from uncultured MAGs where k-mer/LCA tools miss species absent from reference databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-classification--882f6f2cb284e0d1))
- **metagenomic-read-classification**: Use when assigning taxonomic labels to large sets of metagenomic reads (FASTQ/FASTA from SRA accessions such as SRS019120, SRS014468, SRS015055, or Mason-simulated reads), or when benchmarking classifier speed and accuracy against Megablast or MetaPhlAn. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-classification--3f2e5cc62acfee08))
- **metagenomic-read-classification-kraken2**: Reference for applying Kraken 2 to classify 16S amplicon reads from mock community samples at species/genus level and comparing its accuracy against 16S-specific pipelines over matched reference libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-classification-kraken2))
- **metagenomic-read-clustering**: Use when metagenomic Illumina short reads must be split into strain/species-coherent read groups before per-cluster assembly, e.g. as the preprocessing step of an overlap-layout-consensus strain-aware assembly pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-clustering))
- **metagenomic-read-coordinate-processing**: Use when scanning metagenomic FASTA reads with pal-MEM for inverted terminal repeats and you need to encode read coordinates in two-bit space, apply edge-boundary conditions, or exclude MEMs near read ends that arise from amplification artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-coordinate-processing))
- **metagenomic-read-counting-per-taxon**: Use when you already have per-read taxonomic assignments from a MetaCache/MetaCacheSpark query run and need organism-level read counts and abundance percentages at a chosen rank such as species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-counting-per-taxon))
- **metagenomic-read-decontamination**: Use when untargeted Oxford Nanopore FASTQ reads from clinical or animal samples contain host (e.g. human, mouse, mastomys), reagent, or bacterial contamination that must be removed before viral de novo assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-decontamination))
- **metagenomic-read-digital-normalization**: Use when raw paired-end virome FASTQ reads (e.g. SRA runs like SRR3458562 or SRR9875293) feed into VirMAP's mapping assembly, pileup rebuilding, or DIAMOND/BBMap alignment and read depth is uneven or redundant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-digital-normalization))
- **metagenomic-read-gene-annotation**: Use when per-sample DIAMOND tabular hits against a gene catalog (e.g. UniRef90) need -e-value refinement and fractional gene counting to produce gene/orthology abundances from shotgun metagenomic FASTQs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-gene-annotation))
- **metagenomic-read-length-and-n-trimming**: Use when raw or primer-trimmed amplicon FASTQ reads (454 or Illumina) show deteriorating 3'-end quality or embedded Ns and must be trimmed and length-filtered before dereplication and OTU clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-length-and-n-trimming))
- **metagenomic-read-mapping**: Use when metagenomic paired-end FASTQ reads need to be mapped to a multi-genome reference .fasta to quantify genome detection, coverage, microdiversity, or strain sharing (e.g., ZymoBIOMICS mock-community benchmarking). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-mapping))
- **metagenomic-read-mapping-abundance-profiling**: Use when you have predicted MGC/BGC regions from antiSMASH/gutSMASH genomes plus shotgun metagenomic or metatranscriptomic reads (e.g., SRA accessions) and need RPKM abundance profiles and phenotype-differential cluster statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-read-mapping-abundance-profiling))
- **metagenomic-read-mapping-and-counting**: Use when shotgun metagenomic reads (FASTQ) must be quantified against de novo MAG clusters or signature gene sets to estimate organism/subspecies abundances per sample, e.g. tracking B. infantis and B. longum across the COPSAC2010 and CHILD infant cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-mapping-and-counting))
- **metagenomic-read-mapping-quantification**: Use when short-read shotgun metagenomes (FASTQ) must be converted from mapped-read counts into genome-equivalent relative abundances (cell fractions) against a source-associated genome database for source tracking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-mapping-quantification))
- **metagenomic-read-mapping-to-contigs**: Use when you need to judge assembly quality by read support: you have assembled contig FASTA files (individual or merged) and matching trimmed reads, and want per-contig base-coverage percentages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-mapping-to-contigs))
- **metagenomic-read-mapping-to-gene-catalog**: Use when you need to profile the taxonomic and/or functional composition of vaginal metagenomic or metatranscriptomic FASTQ samples against the VIRGO gene catalog instead of assembling reads de novo. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-mapping-to-gene-catalog))
- **metagenomic-read-pair-filtering**: Use when metagenomic reads have been mapped to representative sub-species genomes (e.g. concatenated .fasta) and per-scaffold or per-genome coverage, clonality, nucleotide diversity, or SNV calls must be computed without contamination from falsely mapped read pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-pair-filtering))
- **metagenomic-read-pooling-and-spike-in-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a targeted assembly or annotation pipeline (e.g. CasCollect) against a complete metagenome assembly, and a simulated community with known CRISPR/Cas content and background host contamination is needed from raw FASTQ reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-pooling-and-spike-in-simulation))
- **metagenomic-read-quality-control**: Use when raw metagenomic sequencing FASTQ files must be converted into clean, host-free reads before taxonomic or functional profiling, or when reproducing the Liu 2020 metagenomic pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-quality-control))
- **metagenomic-read-quality-control**: Use when raw paired-end or single-end Illumina metagenomic FASTQ/fastq.gz reads must be adapter-trimmed and quality-trimmed before SSU rRNA read extraction or any 16S/18S-based taxonomic classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-quality-control--333f0c268bb84080))
- **metagenomic-read-quality-control**: Use when raw vaginal metagenomic or metatranscriptomic fastq reads must be cleaned (human/rRNA removal) and mapped to a reference gene catalog such as VIRGO before taxonomic or functional profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-quality-control--b31a2803fda5c4ca))
- **metagenomic-read-quality-filtering**: Use when quality-controlling shotgun metagenomic FASTQs as part of an end-to-end functional annotation pipeline, after host and duplicate read filtering and before mapping reads to a gene database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-quality-filtering))
- **metagenomic-read-quality-trimming**: Use when raw paired-end Illumina shotgun metagenomic reads (e.g. SRA accession SRR606249, 2 x 100 bp) must be cleaned prior to meta-assembly with metaSPAdes or MegaHIT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-quality-trimming))
- **metagenomic-read-query-execution**: Use when benchmarking fimpera-style amplified s-mer querying of a counting Bloom filter on metagenomic FASTQ reads, specifically to verify that query time stays flat or slightly decreases as z increases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-query-execution))
- **metagenomic-read-recruitment-analysis**: Use when evaluating or benchmarking a reference gene catalogue's sequence-capture efficiency on shotgun metagenomic FASTQ samples, or comparing a new catalogue (e.g. VMGC-90) against an incumbent (e.g. VIRGO) via per-sample mapping rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-recruitment-analysis))
- **metagenomic-read-recruitment-targeted-assembly**: Use when you need genomic flanking regions around antimicrobial resistance genes (or other target genes) from short-read metagenomic FASTQ data, and full metagenomic de novo assembly is too computationally expensive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-recruitment-targeted-assembly))
- **metagenomic-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a metagenomic abundance profiler (e.g. BiG-MAP) against known truth: you need simulated shotgun libraries with known gene-cluster abundances to sweep parameter grids (MASH cutoff × Bowtie2 mode) and compute F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-read-simulation))
- **metagenomic-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a taxonomic classifier needs simulated reads with known origin, e.g., ART-generated Illumina/Roche-454 FASTQ sets from genomes whose proteins are excluded from the reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-simulation--c92ebe5455cb3745))
- **metagenomic-read-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic longitudinal metagenomic reads carrying within-strain SNVs and small InDels with configurable allele-frequency trajectories, to benchmark mutation-detection tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-simulation--5c16f40982ea35cf))
- **metagenomic-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking HGT breakpoint detection or local strain reconstruction pipelines against known ground truth, so sensitivity and FDR can be computed from simulated reads at defined coverages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-simulation--c93ca386187e52ce))
- **metagenomic-read-simulation-and-alignment**: Use when benchmarking metagenome binners against curated ground-truth genomes: simulated/sampled per-sample reads must be converted into sorted BAM coverage files and ORF predictions before binning and F1/precision/specificity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-simulation-and-alignment))
- **metagenomic-read-subsampling**: Use when a genome in a simulated community is assembled at extreme coverage (e.g. &gt;1000x) and you need to confirm that coverage — not biology — drives the observed misassembled blocks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-subsampling))
- **metagenomic-read-subsampling-replication**: Use when a user wants to quantify how classification sensitivity and reconstruction completeness degrade with sequencing depth, e.g. testing a pipeline on SRA mock-community reads (FASTQ) at 10/1/0.1% subsampling across replicate trials. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-subsampling-replication))
- **metagenomic-read-taxonomic-attribution**: Use when disease-associated genes from MWAS must be assigned to taxa in a shotgun metagenome, e.g., tracing which organisms reads of a specific gene derive from. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-taxonomic-attribution))
- **metagenomic-read-taxonomic-classification**: Use when you have FASTQ read fractions (mapped/unmapped from read-contig alignment) from a HiFi metagenome assembly and need to know whether low-representation taxa are missing from the assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-taxonomic-classification--00cbae473f23d39a))
- **metagenomic-read-taxonomic-classification**: Use when quality-assuring preprocessed short-read FASTQ metagenomic samples prior to assembly: to classify reads against a reference database, detect contamination, and visualize microbial community composition per sample with Krona charts and a MultiQC report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-taxonomic-classification--8620b1f30ea9c1ab))
- **metagenomic-read-to-protein-alignment**: Use when quality-filtered (host-filtered, duplicate-filtered, quality-trimmed) shotgun metagenomic FASTQs must be mapped to a microbial protein gene database like UniRef90 to obtain read-to-gene matches for functional profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-to-protein-alignment))
- **metagenomic-read-type-gating**: Use when a metagenomic run mixes short-read-only, long-read-only, and paired short+long-read samples, and each sample must be routed to the correct assembly path (MEGAHIT vs SPAdes vs hybridSPAdes) before QUAST-based quality comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-read-type-gating))
- **metagenomic-reference-catalog-retrieval**: Use when a study needs large, publicly available, redundancy-filtered reference protein catalogs (e.g. as search database input for HH-suite3/BFD or for catalog size/provenance audits) and the input is metagenomic or metatranscriptomic protein sequence space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-reference-catalog-retrieval))
