- **metagenomic-reference-sequence-comparison**: Use when candidate transferred segments reconstructed from shotgun metagenomics data must be scored against donor/receptor reference segment sequences to measure local alignment similarity and reconstruction accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-reference-sequence-comparison))
- **metagenomic-sample-metadata-stratification**: Use when a Qiita-deposited fecal shotgun metagenomics feature table (n=666 paired SPF fecal samples) with metadata fields genotype/study/sex/cohort must be subset (e.g., Sacrifice cohort, per-study) before PERMANOVA or beta-diversity testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-sample-metadata-stratification))
- **metagenomic-sample-phenotype-classification**: Use when you have a metagenomic taxonomic profile table (MetaPhlAn merged table or QIIME OTU/BIOM) plus sample metadata with class labels, and the goal is to train a classifier or predict the phenotype of unknown samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-sample-phenotype-classification))
- **metagenomic-scaffold-sequence-processing**: Use when input is metagenomic shotgun data or pre-assembled scaftigs with gene predictions (.tab from Prodigal or MetaGeneMark) and the goal is de novo operon prediction without functional or experimental information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-scaffold-sequence-processing))
- **metagenomic-scaffolding-misjoin-detection**: Use when scaffolding metagenomic contigs with paired long-range links and you must decide which contig-pair links are probable interspecies misjoins based on coverage depth and oligonucleotide composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-scaffolding-misjoin-detection))
- **metagenomic-sequence-clustering-and-retraining**: Use when predicting genes in unassembled environmental shotgun reads (e.g. 454 reads from the human gut microbiome) where GC-content alone is a poor proxy for training model parameterization and an unsupervised organism-based grouping plus retraining step is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-sequence-clustering-and-retraining))
- **metagenomic-sequence-phylogenetic-classification**: Use when gene-predicting unassembled metagenomic reads where GC-content is a poor proxy for choosing training genomes, and phylogenetic classification is needed to parameterize per-organism gene prediction models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-sequence-phylogenetic-classification))
- **metagenomic-sequence-preprocessing**: Use when raw FASTA/FASTQ metagenomic reads or training genomes must be transformed into fixed-length k-mer token ID sequences (canonical k-mers, &lt;unk&gt; handling, padding index 0) before DNN embedding and training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-sequence-preprocessing))
- **metagenomic-shared-variant-calling**: Use when you have shotgun metagenomic BAM alignments from many samples and need to identify SNPs shared across the population (e.g., strain-tracking or ARG diversity analysis), not per-sample de novo calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-shared-variant-calling))
- **metagenomic-short-read-assembly**: Use when the input is unassembled paired-end shotgun FASTQ reads (e.g. Illumina HiSeq from SRA studies such as SRP029211 or SRP008047) and scaftigs must be generated before gene and operon prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-short-read-assembly))
- **metagenomic-short-read-assembly**: Use when raw shotgun metagenomic or virome FASTQ reads must be converted into assembled contigs before viral contig mining, e.g. reproducing the Liang et al. 2020 infant stool 59-sample vOTU analysis with MetaPhage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-short-read-assembly))
- **metagenomic-short-read-mapping**: Use when you have reconstructed full-length SSU rRNA sequences (e.g. metagenome_SSU_sequences.fasta from EMIRGE/MetaRib assembly) and need per-sequence relative abundances before 97% VSEARCH clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-short-read-mapping))
- **metagenomic-shotgun-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking strain-level metagenomic variant discovery tools (e.g., MetaQuad, MQuad) and you need simulated shotgun communities with known ground-truth strain mixtures, engineered variants, and per-sample mean depths spanning 3x-35.5x. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-shotgun-read-simulation))
- **metagenomic-snv-pileup-analysis**: Use when you have VAMB-binned MAGs plus original shotgun reads and need to quantify within-sample strain diversity — i.e. which MAG clusters contain multiple variants (mixed alleles) per sample across one or more environments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-snv-pileup-analysis))
- **metagenomic-source-attribution-scoring**: Use when benchmarking metagenomic fecal source tracking predictions against samples with known source composition (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-source-attribution-scoring))
- **metagenomic-strain-phylogeny-reconstruction**: Use when you have per-MAG-cluster signature genes and per-sample read mappings and need phylogenetic trees whose leaves are samples, to associate metadata with subspecies/strain-level differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-strain-phylogeny-reconstruction))
- **metagenomic-subspecies-stratification**: Use when sample-level gene content profiles mapped to VIRGO (summary.Count.txt restricted via 1.taxon.tbl.txt) are available and the goal is to partition samples into within-species MG-subspecies clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-subspecies-stratification))
- **metagenomic-taxa-gene-feature-extraction**: Use when shotgun metagenome reads must be converted into taxon- and gene-level abundance tables and differentially abundant colorectal-cancer-style biomarkers must be identified while correcting compositional bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-taxa-gene-feature-extraction))
- **metagenomic-taxonomic-cladogram-visualization**: Use when you need to visualize taxonomic biomarkers contrasting metagenomic cohorts (e.g., LEfSe output on MetaPhlAn profiles) as a compact circular cladogram with highlighted, sized, and colored clades. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomic-cladogram-visualization))
- **metagenomic-taxonomic-classification**: Use when raw nanopore read data (FASTQ, FAST5, or POD5, optionally gzipped) from a suspected viral sample must be assigned a taxonomic identity as part of a virus surveillance workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomic-classification--064a295d873cec28))
- **metagenomic-taxonomic-classification**: Use when nanopore long-read metagenomic FASTQ reads need per-read taxonomic classification and an interactive visual summary of community composition in a Galaxy-based (NanoGalaxy) workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomic-classification--cc647523f0383800))
- **metagenomic-taxonomic-classification-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare taxonomic classifiers on metagenomic reads with known community composition, computing per-tool F-measure at genus and species level and runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomic-classification-benchmarking))
- **metagenomic-taxonomic-profile-comparison**: Use when two or more taxonomic profilers report identical or near-identical single-value metric scores (e.g., UniFrac) against a gold standard and you must determine whether their taxonomic profiles actually differ per taxon. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomic-profile-comparison))
- **metagenomic-taxonomic-profile-conversion**: Use when you have a LEfSe-format abundance matrix (from merged MetaPhlAn profiles) and/or a LEfSe biomarker result file and need to render them as a GraPhlAn circular phylogeny with clade-size and ring annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomic-profile-conversion))
- **metagenomic-taxonomic-profiling**: Use when shotgun metagenome FASTQ reads (e.g., mesocosm samples from PRJNA1161616 and fecal spike-in slurries from PRJNA1092107) must be converted into Kraken2 taxonomic profiles or kmtricks k-mer count tables to serve as input for FEAST, metaSourceTracker, or decOM source tracking comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomic-profiling))
- **metagenomic-taxonomy-profiling-of-rna-seq-reads**: Use when RNA-seq FASTQ reads must be screened for microbial (bacterial, archaeal, viral) content: host reads are filtered against a STAR reference index and the exogenous remainder is classified taxonomically with kraken2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomy-profiling-of-rna-seq-reads))
- **metagenomic-taxonomy-tree-annotation**: Use when you have a MetaPhlAn/LEfSe-style abundance table or a BIOM-format 16S table (e.g., from QIIME or mothur, such as a saliva microbiome profile) and need a compact circular taxonomy tree highlighting biomarker clades. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metagenomic-taxonomy-tree-annotation))
- **metagenomic-technical-replicate-separation-analysis**: Decision support for choosing Mash sketch and k-mer sizes when k-mer distances must distinguish technical resequencings of one sample from other metagenomes in a large-scale pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-technical-replicate-separation-analysis))
- **metagenomic-variant-matrix-processing**: Use when you have per-sample AD/DP allele counts from cellsnp-lite over shotgun metagenomic (e.g., ARG) loci and need to filter MetaQuad informative variants (num_of_clusters &gt;= 2 in BIC_params.csv) into an AF matrix for Bray-Curtis PCoA and pairwise.adonis group comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomic-variant-matrix-processing))
- **metagenomic-workflow-curation**: Use when a microbiome analysis plan or pipeline audit must name the recommended tool per metagenomic or amplicon phase (quality control, assembly, binning, classification, annotation) as catalogued in the Bharti & Grimm -practice review. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/metagenomic-workflow-curation))
- **metagenomics**: Shotgun metagenomics workflow with host-depletion-aware QC, taxonomic profiling, functional profiling, AMR follow-up, and reproducible community output tables. ([Runchuan-BU/BioClaw](https://github.com/Runchuan-BU/BioClaw/tree/HEAD/container/skills/metagenomics) · ★411 · 5 copies)
- **metagenomics**: Metagenomic analysis — taxonomic profiling, assembly, binning, and functional annotation of microbiomes. ([aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills](https://github.com/aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/metagenomics) · ★68 · 3 copies)
- **metagenomics**: Apply Metagenomics in computational biology and life sciences. Use when analyzing biological data, developing therapeutics, or building bioinformatics pipelines. This skill covers methodologies, tools, and applications in metagenomics. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/bio/metagenomics) · ★1)
- **metagenomics**: Analyze metagenomic data from environmental samples to characterize microbial communities. ([honeyvig/skillsbench](https://github.com/honeyvig/skillsbench/tree/HEAD/shared_workspace/phylogenetic-tree-construction/environment/skills/metagenomics))
- **metagenomics**: Use when analyzing 16S or shotgun microbiome data: host removal, profiling, assembly and binning, contamination controls, compositional statistics, STORMS reporting. Not for isolate genome assembly (bacterial-genome-assembly-annotation). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/metagenomics))
- **metagenomics-abundance**: Species abundance estimation using Bracken with Kraken2 output. Redistributes reads from higher taxonomic levels to species for more accurate estimates. Use when accurate species-level abundances are needed from Kraken2 classification output. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metagenomics-abundance-estimation) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metagenomics-amr-detection**: Detect antimicrobial resistance genes using AMRFinderPlus, ResFinder, and CARD. Screen isolates and metagenomes for resistance determinants. Use when characterizing resistance profiles in clinical isolates, surveillance samples, or metagenomic data. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metagenomics-amr-detection) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metagenomics-analysis**: Microbial community analysis from sequencing data. Trigger on: microbiome, 16S, shotgun metagenomics, OTU, ASV, taxonomic assignment, diversity, microbial community, gut microbiome, alpha diversity, beta diversity, Shannon, Simpson, Bray-Curtis, UniFrac, PERMANOVA, DADA2, QIIME, Silva, Greengenes, GTDB, amplicon sequencing, rarefaction, chimera, denoising, metatranscriptomics, functional profiling, PICRUSt, HUMAnN. ([mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public](https://github.com/mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public/tree/HEAD/skills/domains/metagenomics))
- **metagenomics-blast-searches**: Run remote BLAST searches against NCBI servers via Bio.Blast.NCBIWWW: pick the correct program and database, configure parameters (word size, composition-based statistics, hitlist_size), submit and poll the RID, then parse and interpret hits using Karlin-Altschul E-values and bit-scores. Avoids the max_target_seqs misuse (Shah et al. 2019). ([ai4nucleome/BioMaster](https://github.com/ai4nucleome/BioMaster/tree/HEAD/.opencode/bioskills/metagenomics-blast-searches) · ★125)
- **metagenomics-depth-of-coverage-import**: Use when a tabular metagenomics depth-of-coverage time series (Example 1 / BWWTP data) must be loaded into MOVIS for time-series visualization, and the temporal feature must be confirmed during sanitizing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/metagenomics-depth-of-coverage-import))
- **metagenomics-functional-profiling**: Profile functional potential of metagenomes using HUMAnN3 and similar tools. Use when obtaining pathway abundances, gene family counts, or functional annotations from metagenomic data. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metagenomics-functional-profiling) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metagenomics-kraken**: Taxonomic classification of metagenomic reads using Kraken2. Fast k-mer based classification against RefSeq database. Use when performing initial taxonomic classification of shotgun metagenomic reads before abundance estimation with Bracken. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metagenomics-kraken-classification) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metagenomics-llm**: Route genomic/protein language models and Steinegger-lab structure/sequence search for shotgun metagenomics. Use when: (1) Choosing gLMs vs PLMs vs classical homology, (2) MMseqs2 vs Foldseek vs PLM search, (3) Spacedust / AlphaGEM, (4) Distinguishing foundation-model corpora from SRA/MGnify reanalysis. For DNA gLMs use genome-language-model; for protein LMs use protein-language-model (esm, esmc, saprot, prostt5, plmsearch, deepblast). Classical pipelines: metagenomics-workflow / tool-selection. ([PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills](https://github.com/PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/metagenomics-llm) · ★7)
- **metagenomics-metaphlan**: Marker gene-based taxonomic profiling using MetaPhlAn 4. Provides accurate species-level relative abundances using clade-specific markers. Use when accurate taxonomic profiling is needed and computational resources are limited, or for comparison with HMP/other MetaPhlAn studies. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metagenomics-metaphlan-profiling) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metagenomics-microbiome**: Activate FIRST for Shotgun/amplicon taxa, eDNA, diversity/phylogenies and microbial dynamics. Then READ its selection guide, OPEN the chosen method's SKILL.md, and FOLLOW that method's documented steps/scripts before writing any analysis or Bash. ([canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang](https://github.com/canquedehuiyi-cpu/xiongbao-nenglishichang/tree/HEAD/plugins/market/Knowledge/bio-research-guided/skills/metagenomics-microbiome))
- **metagenomics-pipeline**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Workflow_Management/workflows/metagenomics-pipeline) · ★7)
- **metagenomics-router**: Use for any metagenomics how-to question (which method, tool, parameters or pipeline to use), before answering from memory. Searches 2,754 evidence-grounded metagenomics skills distilled from method papers and their code, 2 end-to-end workflows and 717 tool records, then reads the one that fits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/skills/_router))
- **metagenomics-strain-tracking**: Track bacterial strains using MASH, sourmash, fastANI, and inStrain. Compare genomes, detect contamination, and monitor strain-level variation. Use when needing sub-species resolution for outbreak tracking, transmission analysis, or within-host strain dynamics. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metagenomics-strain-tracking) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metagenomics-visualization**: Visualize metagenomic profiles using R (phyloseq, microbiome) and Python (matplotlib, seaborn). Create stacked bar plots, heatmaps, PCA plots, and diversity analyses. Use when creating publication-quality figures from MetaPhlAn, Bracken, or other taxonomic profiling output. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metagenomics-metagenome-visualization) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metagenomics-workflow**: End-to-end shotgun metagenomics: quality control, assembly, mapping, binning, taxonomic annotation, functional annotation, microbial mining (BGCs, AMR, MGEs, element cycling, DOM, defense), and research analysis (stats/figures). Use when starting a project or ordering stages. For tool choice use tool-selection. For mining use microbial-mining; for paper stats use research-analysis. For public DBs use public-databases. ([PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills](https://github.com/PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/metagenomics-workflow) · ★7)
- **metagenomics-workflow-router**: Use when a user has a whole metagenomics analysis goal rather than a single atomic step and needs an end-to-end composite workflow selected and grounded against source papers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/workflows/_workflow_router))
- **metagent**: Analyze tooling, MCP/plugin availability, skill portfolios, and instruction ownership. Use when diagnosing tool or namespace availability; auditing skill provenance, overlap, use, or lifecycle; or deciding where guidance belongs. ([ianwatts22/metagent](https://github.com/ianwatts22/metagent/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/metagent) · ★1)

- **metagit-aos**: Compose ACL, task graph, scheduler, merge, and related coordination subsystems via metagit aos|coord status|doctor|next. - **metagit-bootstrap**: Create or refine a local .metagit.yml. Default is deterministic `metagit init`; do not dump detect payloads into context. ([metagit-ai/metagit-cli](https://github.com/metagit-ai/metagit-cli/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/metagit-bootstrap) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **metagit-campaign**: Plan and track cross-project multi-repo campaigns — YAML overlays, repo rollups, objective fan-out, and MR status. Use for umbrella-scale coordinated work. ([metagit-ai/metagit-cli](https://github.com/metagit-ai/metagit-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/metagit-campaign) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **metagit-cli**: CLI-only shortcuts for metagit agents — workspace catalog, discovery, prompts, sync, layout, and config. - **metagit-config-refresh**: Refresh or bootstrap `.metagit.yml` using deterministic init/validate. Use when configuration is missing, stale, or incomplete for workspace operations. - **metagit-context-pack**: Token-efficient workspace onboarding via tiered context packs — map, repo cards, session digest, objectives, approvals, repomix profiles. - **metagit-control-center**: Use when running metagit as an MCP control center for multi-repo awareness, guarded sync, and operational knowledge across ongoing agent tasks. - **metagit-gating**: Use when implementing or operating Metagit MCP server activation and tool exposure rules based on .metagit.yml presence and validity. ([metagit-ai/metagit-cli](https://github.com/metagit-ai/metagit-cli/tree/HEAD/src/metagit/data/skills/metagit-gating) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **metagit-gitnexus**: Run gitnexus analysis for a target workspace and selected project repositories before graph-dependent tasks.
